### [1] [Fidelity Preference, Not Demographic Preference: A Pixel-Level Attribute-Sensitivity Audit of Image Aesthetic/Preference Scorers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23593)
*Mingyang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文审计了四种文本到图像系统的美学评分器（LAION-Aesthetics、PickScore、ImageReward、HPSv2），通过对肤色和体型进行像素级干预，发现这些评分器主要表现出保真度偏好（未修改图像得分最高，任何方向的扰动都会受到惩罚），而非明确的人口统计学偏见。关键发现是合成图像审计具有误导性——结果在真实图像上会反转或减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像系统广泛使用美学评分器来过滤训练数据和引导生成过程，但这些评分是否将人口统计学属性编码为客观质量尚未明确。现有的审计方法（主要基于合成图像）可能得出误导性结论，因此需要更严谨、可复现的审计框架来区分真实的人口统计学偏见与保真度偏好。

Method: 对四个美学评分器使用像素级干预方法，在合成和真实图像上对肤色（CIELAB L*亮度）和体型进行扰动。设置安慰剂对照组（对非皮肤区域施加相同的L*偏移）来排除皮肤操作符的影响。在1470张真实面孔上进行交叉验证，并采用FDR校正进行人群分层分析。提出了像素级因果隔离的审计性标准。

Result: 发现主导效应是保真度偏好（倒U形曲线），而非人口统计学偏好。该罚项不是皮肤操作符的伪影（安慰剂组结果相似），但依赖操作符。合成图像审计具有误导性：LAION-Aes在合成面孔上偏爱较深肤色，但在真实面孔上偏好反转且幅度大幅减小。跨评分器的合成结果不可迁移。FDR校正后保真度罚项的不对称性仅在HPSv2中稳健。

Conclusion: 天真的合成图像审计会误判偏见的方向和幅度。只有在对真实数据上进行图像内因果隔离，才能区分真实的人口统计学偏见与保真度偏好。该工作提供了带伪影控制的可复现基准以及像素级因果隔离的审计性标准。

Abstract: Text-to-image systems use learned aesthetic scorers to filter training data and guide generation, but whether these scores encode demographic attributes as objective quality is unclear. We audit four scorers (LAION-Aesthetics, PickScore, ImageReward, HPSv2) using pixel-level interventions on skin tone and body type in synthetic and real images. Our key finding is that along skin-lightness, the dominant effect is fidelity preference: unaltered images score highest, and perturbations in either direction are penalized (inverted-U). Placebo arms show this penalty is not an artifact of the skin operator, as applying the same CIELAB L* shift to non-skin regions yields similar penalty magnitudes. However, the penalty is operator-dependent and holds for all operators only for LAION-Aes. Critically, audits on synthetic images alone are misleading: LAION-Aes shows strong preference for darker skin on synthetic faces, but on 1470 real faces the preference reverses and becomes much smaller, and amplification becomes non-significant. Across scorers, synthetic results do not transfer -- reversing for LAION-Aes and HPSv2, attenuating for PickScore. We contribute a reproducible benchmark with artifact control and synthetic/real cross-validation, and an auditability criterion for pixel-level causal isolation (valid for skin tone, not for body type due to deformation). Population-stratified analysis shows fidelity-penalty asymmetry is not robust across groups after FDR correction except for HPSv2. Our findings show naive synthetic audits misjudge bias direction and magnitude, and only within-image causal isolation on real data can distinguish true demographic bias from fidelity preference.

</details>


### [2] [The Blending Ratio Is Not Where the Performance Is: Diagnosing Prototype Blending for Few-Shot Adaptation of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23634)
*Liangzhi Li,Bowen Wang,Yiming Qian,Thorsten Neumann,Xia Xie,Guangshun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了视觉-语言模型少样本适应中零样本文本原型与K个标注图像特征均值之间的混合比例问题。作者发现：理论上最优的比例（闭式解，即正部James-Stein收缩系数）在4800个实验单元中平均比测试集oracle比例落后8.5个点，因为78%的文本-图像原型距离是argmax基本抵消的类无关偏移。留一法可在无验证数据下将比例调到距oracle仅0.9点。更重要的是，无验证线性探针（CLAP、LP++）甚至击败了oracle调参的混合方法，说明性能天花板在模型类本身而非超参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少样本适应方法通常采用零样本文本原型和标注图像特征均值的凸组合，但混合比例常依赖测试集标签调参。作者质疑这一族方法的偏差-方差理论基础能否指导找到正确比例，以及该比例能否无需验证数据估计、是否真的是性能瓶颈所在。

Method: 推导最小化原型均方误差的比例闭式解（支持集代入后恰为正部James-Stein收缩系数）；在10数据集×5骨干（含SigLIP）×5样本数×5种子×4提示层级共4800个单元上系统评测；证明文本-图像原型距离中类无关偏移成分的存在与占比；提出留一法估计混合比例；对比多种无验证线性探针基线。

Result: 理论上最优比例在950个primary-tier单元上比测试集oracle比例落后8.5点且趋向饱和为1（丢弃文本先验）；78%的原型距离为类无关偏移，反事实界定其损伤占比26%。留一法估计的比例距oracle仅0.9点。无验证线性探针CLAP平均+1.9点、LP++平均+1.5点超越oracle混合；K>=4时四个无验证基线均高于oracle。

Conclusion: 混合比例可以近乎最优地免费估计，但性能天花板并不在超参数调优上，而在模型类本身——线性探针无需验证数据即可超越oracle调参的混合分类器。这一定位结论为该少样本适应范式提供了重要的方法论指引。

Abstract: Many few-shot adaptation methods for vision-language models classify with a convex combination of the zero-shot text prototype and the mean of the K labelled image features, with a single blending ratio routinely tuned on held-out labels, often on the test set itself. We ask what the family's own bias-variance justification invites: what is the right ratio, can it be estimated without validation data, and is finding it where the performance is? First, the ratio minimising prototype mean-squared error has a closed form whose support-set plug-in is exactly a positive-part James-Stein coefficient shrinking towards the text prototype. Across 4,800 cells (ten datasets, five backbones including SigLIP, five shot counts, five seeds, four prompt tiers) this theoretically optimal ratio is a reliable estimate of the wrong quantity: on the 950 primary-tier cells where it is defined it trails a test-set-oracle ratio by 8.5 points. It saturates near 1, discarding the text prior for a nearest-class-mean classifier, because 78% of the text-image prototype distance it treats as bias is a class-independent offset that the arg max largely cancels. We prove the mechanism and bound its share of the damage at 26% by a counterfactual. Second, leave-one-out on the support set alone sets a ratio landing within 0.9 points of the oracle blend, so it is estimable without validation data. Third, validation-free linear probes beat even the oracle-tuned blend: CLAP by +1.9 points and LP++ by +1.5 on average, and at K >= 4 all four validation-free baselines sit above the oracle, the linear probes by margins excluding zero. These results locate the ceiling in the model class, not the hyperparameter: the ratio can be set near-optimally for free, and it is still not where the performance is. Code, cached features, per-cell records: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Liangzhi-Li/clipbench-blending

</details>


### [3] [Cross-Generation Optimization of YOLOv26, YOLOv11, and YOLOv8 for Fine-Grained Small-Object Detection and Instance Segmentation in Complex Orchards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23636)
*Ranjan Sapkota,Manoj Karkee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究对Ultralytics YOLOv8、YOLOv11和YOLOv26三个版本的模型进行了跨代基准测试，用于果园环境中苹果幼果、花萼和果柄的检测与实例分割任务。通过五种模型规模（n、s、m、l、x）和两种训练分辨率（640×640和960×960）共30组实验，发现YOLOv11s-960取得最佳性能（mask mAP@50:95为0.402），而YOLOv26s-960在参数更少（10.37M）的情况下取得相近性能，果柄是最具挑战性的类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 果园环境中的小目标检测和实例分割面临绿色背景相似性、遮挡以及果实质地精细结构像素表示有限的挑战，需要系统性评估不同代YOLO模型在该场景下的表现，为机器人果园感知提供实用基准。

Method: 对YOLOv8、YOLOv11和YOLOv26三个模型版本各五个规模（n、s、m、l、x），在640×640和960×960两种训练配置下进行交叉评估，共30组实验，检测和分割苹果幼果、花萼和果柄三种结构。

Result: YOLOv11s-960取得最高mask mAP@50:95（0.402）和box mAP@50:95（0.426）；YOLOv26s-960以10.37M参数和34.1 GFLOPs取得mAP 0.397和0.425的相近性能；果柄是最具挑战性的类别；增大模型容量未持续提升精度。

Conclusion: 紧凑到中等规模的YOLO模型配合小目标聚焦训练能够在精度与效率之间取得更优平衡，为精细农业机器人和果园感知建立了实用的基准。

Abstract: Small-object detection and instance segmentation remain challenging in orchard environments because of green-on-green similarity, occlusion, and limited pixel representation of fine fruit anatomy. This study presents a cross-generation benchmark of Ultralytics YOLOv8, YOLOv11, and YOLOv26 for detecting and segmenting apple fruitlet, calyx, and peduncle structures for robotic orchard perception. Five model scales (n, s, m, l, and x) were evaluated under conventional 640 x 640 and small-object focused 960 x 960 training configurations, yielding 30 experiments. Increasing model capacity did not consistently improve accuracy. YOLOv11s-960 achieved the highest observed mask mAP@50:95 (0.402) and box mAP@50:95 (0.426), while YOLOv26s-960 achieved comparable values of 0.397 and 0.425 with only 10.37 M parameters and 34.1 GFLOPs. Peduncle remained the most challenging class. Overall, compact-to-moderate YOLO models with small-object-focused training provided favorable accuracy efficiency trade-offs, establishing a practical benchmark for fine-grained agricultural robotics and orchard perception. Github Link: https://github.com/rnjnspkt/Optimizing-and-Comparing-Ultralytics-YOLOv26-YOLOv11-and-YOLOv8-for-Small-Object-Detection-and-Seg

</details>


### [4] [Scaling Reinforcement Learning for Diffusion Models via Velocity Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23664)
*Jaemoo Choi,Wei Guo,Yuchen Zhu,Arash Vahdat,Molei Tao,Julius Berner,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出奖励速度匹配（RVM），一种无需轨迹的扩散模型奖励微调方法，直接在速度场上进行更新，规避了传统基于似然的策略梯度方法在扩散模型上计算复杂的问题，并在多个大规模任务上以更低的训练成本取得有竞争力的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型奖励微调方法大多沿用大语言模型的策略梯度机制，但扩散模型无法提供生成样本的可计算似然，导致现有方法要么构建随机去噪轨迹的似然，要么用ELBO近似端点似然，引入额外计算和算法复杂性。这促使作者探索是否可以在不依赖似然机制的情况下实现有效的扩散奖励微调。

Method: 提出奖励速度匹配（RVM）：一种简单、免轨迹的更新方法，直接作用于速度场（velocity field）。其核心机制是强化与高奖励生成相关的方向、抑制低奖励方向，并可选地包含锚定项以控制与参考速度的漂移。RVM是一个通用框架，可将RAM和DiffusionNFT等近期方法作为特例恢复。

Result: 在多个大规模扩散模型奖励微调任务中，RVM在显著降低训练成本的情况下，与基于轨迹的策略梯度方法相当或更优。实验还发现，简化速度更新后，损失变体的选择不如奖励和锚定设计重要。对于视频生成，标准偏好奖励倾向于产生视觉干净但近乎静态的输出，而引入新的动态追踪奖励可大幅改善运动表现并提升整体VBench性能。

Conclusion: 可扩展的扩散模型奖励微调更适合在原生速度表征上进行，而非作为基于似然的策略优化问题。这一结论为扩散模型对齐提供了更简洁有效的路径。

Abstract: Reward fine-tuning is becoming an important tool for adapting diffusion models to human preferences and task-specific objectives, but existing methods largely inherit policy-gradient machinery from large language models. Unlike autoregressive models, diffusion models do not provide tractable likelihoods for generated samples. As a result, current approaches either construct trajectory likelihoods from stochastic denoising transitions or approximate endpoint likelihoods with evidence lower bound, introducing additional computation and algorithmic complexity. We demonstrate that this likelihood-based machinery is not necessary for effective diffusion reward fine-tuning. We propose reward-based velocity matching (RVM), a simple trajectory-free update that acts directly on the velocity field. RVM reinforces directions associated with high-reward generations, suppresses those with low reward, and involves an optional anchor term controlling drift from a reference velocity. Notably, it provides a general framework that recovers recent fine-tuning methods, including RAM and DiffusionNFT, as special cases. Across various large-scale diffusion models reward fine-tuning tasks, RVM is competitive with or outperforms trajectory-based policy-gradient methods under substantially reduced training cost. We further find that, once the velocity update is simplified, the particular loss variant matters less than reward and anchor design. For video generation, standard preference rewards can favor visually clean but nearly static outputs; introducing a new dynamic-tracking reward that substantially improve motions while improving overall VBench performance. These results suggest that scalable reward fine-tuning for diffusion models is better posed in the native velocity representation than as likelihood-based policy optimization.

</details>


### [5] [Platonic Representation Hypothesis on World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23720)
*Wenhow Li,Chengwei MA,Hui Xiong,Ying-Cong Chen,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在World Model领域探究了Platonic Representation Hypothesis，提出预测一致性假设（Predictive Consistency Assumption），并通过DINO-WM系统实验证明不同世界模型在优化共享状态转移目标时，会趋向收敛到几何相似且功能兼容的共享潜在结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管世界模型在感知和模拟复杂环境方面表现出强大能力，但其学习到的表征的本质仍未被充分理解。作者希望探究不同世界模型是否在优化预测目标的过程中自发收敛到共享的潜在结构。

Method: 提出预测一致性假设，即共享状态转移目标的优化作为一种选择性压力，促使异构模型收敛到共享潜在结构。通过DINO-WM系统实验，变化视觉编码器构建异构模型，并结合模型拼接（model stitching）技术验证跨模型特征映射的功能兼容性。

Result: 能力强的世界模型会演化出几何相似的内部结构；通过模型拼接，一个世界模型的内部特征可以映射到另一个模型，且性能下降有限，证明了功能兼容性。

Conclusion: 追求预测一致性可以促进世界模型之间形成共享的、转移兼容的潜在结构，为理解世界模型表征的本质提供了实证支持。

Abstract: World models have demonstrated significant potential for perceiving and simulating complex environments. Despite their strong performance, the fundamental nature of their learned representations remains poorly understood. In this paper, we investigate the Platonic Representation Hypothesis within this domain by proposing the Predictive Consistency Assumption: we posit that the optimization of a shared state transition objective acts as a selective pressure that encourages heterogeneous models to converge toward a shared latent structure. Through systematic experiments with the DINO World Model (DINO-WM), in which we vary visual encoders to create heterogeneous models, we find that capable world models evolve toward geometrically similar internal structures. Moreover, via model stitching, we show that the internal features of one world model can be mapped to another with limited performance degradation, providing evidence of functional compatibility. Our findings suggest that the pursuit of predictive consistency can promote shared, transition-compatible latent structure across world models.

</details>


### [6] [DriftAD: Visually-Guided Text Drift for Few-Shot Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23723)
*Wenyang Liu,Tianyi Liu,Dongshuo Zhang,Kejun Wu,Adams Wai-Kin Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DriftAD框架，通过视觉引导的文本漂移机制，将CLIP的静态文本嵌入动态转换为分层、空间自适应的异常描述符，以解决少样本异常检测中全局到局部匹配刚性不足的问题。实验在MVTec-AD和VisA上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FSAD方法依赖静态文本提示，将其均匀应用于整个特征层级和空间维度。这种刚性全局到局部匹配无法捕获工业缺陷高度局部化且尺度依赖的物理变化特征。

Method: 提出三个核心模块：(1) ASA异常信号放大模块，通过空间和频率分支增强微弱缺陷信号；(2) VGTD视觉引导文本漂移模块，动态转换冻结的CLIP文本嵌入，生成逐层、空间自适应的异常描述符；(3) DGSG漂移引导空间门控模块，利用漂移后的异常描述符作为空间探针选择性增强异常相关视觉特征。另设计漂移分离损失和门控监督损失防止表示坍缩并保证空间判别性。

Result: 在MVTec-AD和VisA数据集上，1-shot、2-shot和4-shot设置的图像级与像素级指标均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 通过让文本描述符随局部视觉上下文动态漂移，DriftAD有效解决了少样本异常检测中静态文本与分层视觉特征匹配不充分的问题，显著提升了检测精度。

Abstract: Few-shot anomaly detection (FSAD) has recently benefited from vision-language models such as CLIP, which enable anomaly de?tection by aligning visual features with text descriptions of normal and abnormal states. However, existing methods typically rely on static text prompts that are applied uniformly across the entire feature hierarchy and spatial dimensions. This rigid global-to-local matching fails to capture the highly localized and scale-dependent physical variations of industrial defects. To address this, we propose DriftAD, a FSAD framework built on three key modules. First, an Anomaly Signal Amplification (ASA) module enhances subtle defect signals through spatial and frequency branches before text-visual matching. Second, Visually-Guided Text Drift (VGTD) dynamically transforms frozen CLIP text embeddings, steering them into layer?wise, spatially-adaptive anomaly descriptors conditioned on local visual context at each encoder depth. Third, Drift-Guided Spatial Gating (DGSG) uses the drifted abnormal descriptor as a spatial probe to selectively enhance anomaly-relevant visual features. Addi?tionally, a drift separation loss prevents representational collapse of the drifted descriptors, and a gate supervision loss enforces spatially discriminative gating in DGSG. Extensive experiments on MVTec?AD and VisA demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across all 1-, 2-, and 4-shot settings on both image-level and pixel-level metrics. Code is available at https://github.com/wenyang001/DriftAD.

</details>


### [7] [Velocity-coupled Representation Refinement for Satellite Orbit Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23728)
*Yue Yang,Zhiqiang Wu,Saiyu Qi,Fan Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OrbitNet，一种速度感知的表示学习方法用于卫星轨道预测。它通过位置-速度耦合表示学习范式，利用交叉变量交互增强位置表示，并引入轨道分段建模捕获局部运动变化和长期演化模式，在Starlink数据集和六个未见星座的零样本评估中均超越大型时间序列基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有卫星轨道预测方法主要关注位置序列的时间依赖，忽略了位置与速度之间固有的耦合关系，而这种耦合对于建模卫星运动至关重要。

Method: 1) 提出速度耦合表示细化策略，通过位置与速度的交叉变量交互增强位置表示；2) 引入轨道分段建模，将历史轨迹分割为时间片段并进行片段级时间学习，同时捕获局部运动变化和长期演化模式。

Result: 在Starlink域内评估和六个未见卫星星座的零样本评估中，OrbitNet均优于大型时间序列基础模型和代表性通用预测方法。

Conclusion: 位置-速度耦合表示学习范式能够有效提升卫星轨道预测精度，该工作鼓励进一步探索卫星感知的表示学习方法用于轨迹时间序列预测。

Abstract: Satellite orbit prediction, which aims to forecast future orbital trajectories from historical observations, is important for collision warning and safe space operations. With advances in time-series forecasting, learning-based methods have emerged as a promising solution for satellite prediction. In orbital dynamics, a satellite state is typically described by position and velocity, where position characterizes trajectory geometry and velocity reflects its instantaneous direction and rate of change. However, most existing methods mainly focus on temporal dependencies within position sequences while rarely exploiting the intrinsic coupling between position and velocity, which is essential for modeling satellite motion. To this end, we propose OrbitNet, a velocity-aware representation learning method for accurate satellite orbit prediction. It lifts conventional position-sequence forecasting to a position-velocity coupled representation learning paradigm by exploiting relationships among satellite state variables. Specifically, we develop a velocity-coupled representation refinement strategy to enhance positional representations through cross-variable interactions between position and velocity. We further introduce orbital segment modeling, which partitions historical trajectories into temporal segments and performs segment-level temporal learning to capture local motion variations and long-range evolution patterns. Extensive experiments show that OrbitNet outperforms large time-series foundation models and representative general forecasting methods under both in-domain evaluation on Starlink and zero-shot evaluation across six unseen satellite constellations. We expect this work to encourage further exploration of satellite-aware representation learning for trajectory time-series forecasting.

</details>


### [8] [More Motion Is Not Always Better Motion: Corpus Composition Governs Whether Augmentation Helps SMPL-Based Parkinsonian Gait Severity Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23730)
*Michael Caiola,Andrew C. Weitz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文使用三个冻结的MotionAGFormer编码器从SMPL动作中评估MDS-UPDRS步态严重程度，在隐藏的多中心测试集上达到macro-F1 0.58。通过隔离步态语料库的贡献，发现决定性能的关键不是数据量，而是语料库中是否包含步态速度的对比（速度变化），而非速度变化本身；添加第三个采集点反而更差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究从SMPL动作表征中评估帕金森病步态严重程度时，不同训练语料库对模型性能的影响，特别是理解为什么某些语料库有效而其他无效。

Method: 使用三个仅在提升语料库上不同的冻结MotionAGFormer编码器作为特征提取器，在隐藏的多中心测试集上分别单独评估每个编码器，以隔离语料库对性能的贡献。构建六个来自同一惯性数据集、仅改变步行任务组成的池，并进行对比实验。

Result: 六个池得分在0.32到0.53之间，仅有一个超过未使用外部运动数据的编码器（0.51）。关键因素是语料库中是否存在步态速度对比，而非数据量大小。添加第三个采集点反而降低性能。合成运动和单目重建网络视频均无法帮助。

Conclusion: 语料库中步态速度的对比是表示学习性能的关键因素，而非数据量。修改学习表示本身的各种尝试均失败，表明应从语料库设计（引入速度变化）而非模型架构入手改进。

Abstract: We grade MDS-UPDRS gait severity from SMPL motion using three frozen MotionAGFormer encoders as featurizers, reaching macro-F1 0.58 on a hidden, multi-site test set. Because the system's members differ only in their lifting corpus, evaluating encoders singly on that test set isolates what that corpus contributes. Six pools drawn from one inertial dataset, varying only in which walking tasks they include, score between 0.32 and 0.53, and just one of them beats the 0.51 of an encoder given no outside motion at all. What separates them is not how much data they hold but whether they carry a contrast in walking speed, the variation this representation appears to depend : a further pool adding a third collection site at fixed task composition does worse still. The same rule explains why exact synthetic motion and monocularly reconstructed web video both fail to help. Modifying the learned representation itself, rather than the corpus behind it, cost every variant that attempted it.

</details>


### [9] [CRISP: Calibration-Aware Visual State Space Duality for Remote Sensing Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23746)
*Kangning Wang,Haopeng Zhang,Zhiguo Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CRISP是一个针对遥感语义分割的校准框架，解决VSSD（视觉状态空间对偶）模型在全局聚合时抑制高频响应、导致边界过度平滑的问题。其核心包括Duality Calibration Operator（DCO）通过残差注入和频率校准恢复局部对比与边界响应（保持线性复杂度），以及Orthogonal Multi-Prototype（OMP）头用正交约束的多原型建模类内方差。在Potsdam、Vaihingen和LoveDA上，约30M参数下mF和mIoU均稳定提升，与SOTA竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VSSD等状态空间模型虽为密集视觉任务提供了线性时间的高效替代方案，但作者观察到VSSD将空间上下文压缩为全局聚合，抑制了高频响应，导致遥感语义分割中边界过度平滑的问题。

Method: 提出CRISP校准框架，包含两个组件：1) 核心Duality Calibration Operator（DCO），通过残差注入和频率校准在VSSD骨干内恢复局部对比和边界响应，且不改变其线性复杂度；2) Orthogonal Multi-Prototype（OMP）头，为每个类别分配多个正交约束的原型，以建模大的类内方差并保留恢复的细节。

Result: 在Potsdam、Vaihingen和LoveDA三个数据集上的大量实验表明，CRISP以约30M参数在平均F1（mF）和mIoU上取得了一致的增益，与现有最先进方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: CRISP有效解决了VSSD在遥感分割中的边界平滑缺陷，通过轻量级校准同时保持线性复杂度，展示了状态空间模型在细节敏感任务上的可增强性。

Abstract: State space models, especially Visual State Space Duality (VSSD), have emerged as efficient linear-time alternatives to Transformers for dense visual tasks. However, we observe that VSSD compresses spatial context into a global aggregation that suppresses high-frequency responses, causing excessive boundary smoothing in remote sensing semantic segmentation. To address this, we propose CRISP, a calibration framework with two components. Its core, the Duality Calibration Operator (DCO), restores local contrast and boundary responses through residual injection and frequency calibration within the VSSD backbone, without altering its linear complexity. To retain the recovered detail, an Orthogonal Multi-Prototype (OMP) head assigns multiple orthogonally constrained prototypes per class to model large intra-class variance. Extensive experiments on Potsdam, Vaihingen, and LoveDA show that, with approximately 30M parameters, CRISP achieves consistent gains in mean F1 (mF) and mIoU while remaining competitive with state-of-the-art methods. Code is available at https://github.com/crazylifeha/CRISP.

</details>


### [10] [Too much of a good thing -- when knowledge distillation promotes overfitting, and how to avoid it](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23752)
*Irene Trigueros-Lorca,Leonardo Concepción,Christian Wagner,Isaac Triguero,Daniel Molina*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于同构块的中间层知识蒸馏方法，在经典数据集上仅需最后块蒸馏即可，而在细粒度小样本场景下，中间层监督能显著提升学生模型的性能与数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏（KD）传统上仅应用于网络最终输出层，中间层逐块蒸馏的行为未被充分研究。作者想探究在细粒度数据集常见的小样本场景下，中间层块级监督是否能带来优势。

Method: 设计一种学生网络，由与教师网络对应的简单同构块组成，在对应块之间进行知识蒸馏。通过注意力图、Centered Kernel Alignment 和 Grad-CAM 等可解释性分析来指导监督配置的选择，并研究不同粒度的蒸馏配置及教师/学生的微调策略。

Result: 在11个数据集上验证：经典数据集上仅蒸馏最后一块通常足够且最优；而在细粒度、样本稀缺场景下，中间层监督带来显著收益，即使只增加一个中间蒸馏点也能大幅缩小性能差距。

Conclusion: 适当引导的中间层块级蒸馏是构建紧凑且数据高效模型的关键手段，能在不牺牲准确率的前提下提升学生模型在小样本细粒度任务上的表现。

Abstract: The growing size of Convolutional Neural Networks has led to increasingly large and costly models. Knowledge Distillation (KD) addresses this by transferring knowledge from a large network (teacher) to a small one (student), also reducing the training data required. KD is traditionally applied only at the network's final output. However, its behaviour when applied at intermediate network layers has received little attention. This raises the question of whether intermediate block-wise KD, which provides supervision throughout the network, could offer an advantage under specific conditions, such as few instances per class, which is common in fine-grained datasets. This work proposes a student design based on simple, homogeneous blocks mirroring those of the teacher, distilling knowledge between corresponding blocks. Across eleven datasets, we show that on classic datasets, distilling only the last block is sufficient -- and often best--, whereas fine-grained, data-scarce settings benefit substantially from intermediate supervision, with even a single additional distillation point narrowing the gap considerably. We further study how this supervision should be guided, exploring configurations of varying granularity and informed by an explainability analysis based on attention maps, Centered Kernel Alignment, and Grad-CAM, alongside the impact of teacher and student fine-tuning strategies. This work shows that intermediate block-wise distillation, guided appropriately, is key to building compact data-efficient models without sacrificing accuracy.

</details>


### [11] [Primate vision reveals a missing principle for robust dynamic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23790)
*Matteo Dunnhofer,Christian Micheloni,Kohitij Kar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过对比人类感知、猕猴下颞叶皮层（IT）神经活动与多种图像/视频神经网络表征，研究视觉系统如何整合物体外观与运动信息并保持对外观变化的鲁棒性。发现预测世界模型兼具强跨外观泛化能力和最高神经保真度，但没有任何模型能复现皮层从早期外观主导到后期外观不变运动编码的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解智能视觉系统如何将物体外观与运动信息整合，同时在外观变化时保持鲁棒性——这是一个尚未解决的神经科学和人工智能核心问题。

Method: 对比人类感知和猕猴IT皮层神经活动与多种神经网络表征，包括图像/视频模型（识别、分割、光流处理、预测世界建模），测试其跨外观泛化能力和神经一致性。

Result: 时间整合改善了物体表征，但多数视频识别模型在外观破坏而运动结构保留时泛化能力差；人类和猕猴IT保持鲁棒。预测世界模型在跨外观泛化和神经保真度上均最优，但无模型能复现皮层早期外观主导向晚期外观不变运动编码的转换。

Conclusion: 运动信息逐步整合进物体表征是鲁棒动态视觉的神经原理，预测学习是实现在人工系统中该计算的可行途径。

Abstract: How does an intelligent visual system combine what objects look like with how they move while remaining robust as appearance changes? We addressed this question by comparing human perception and neural activity in macaque inferior temporal cortex with representations from image- and video-based neural networks spanning recognition, segmentation, optic-flow processing and predictive world modeling. Temporal integration improved object representations, but most video recognition models generalized poorly when appearance was disrupted while motion structure was preserved. Humans and macaque IT remained robust. Notably, predictive world models combined strong cross-appearance generalization with the closest correspondence to IT, outperforming other video-modeling approaches in neural fidelity. Yet no model reproduced the cortical transformation from early appearance-dominated responses toward later appearance-invariant motion coding. These results identify progressive integration of motion into object representations as a principle of robust dynamic vision and implicate predictive learning as a promising route toward realizing this computation in artificial systems.

</details>


### [12] [Restoring Without Forgetting: Continual Learning Across Image Degradations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23799)
*Alif Ashrafee,Bartosz Krawczyk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RwF（Restoring without Forgetting）框架，将多退化图像复原建模为持续域增量学习问题。通过为每个新退化学习轻量适配器来从根本上消除灾难性遗忘，并设计无监督路由机制在测试时识别合适的复原路径，无需领域标签。在五个退化域序列上，相较于朴素顺序微调，在Restormer和NAFNet主干上分别提升了15.25 dB和11.83 dB的平均PSNR，并在11个真实退化基准上达到89.5%的路由准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全合一图像复原架构虽在静态基准上有效，但假设训练时可同时获取所有目标退化，属于封闭世界设定。实际部署中退化是顺序出现的，且历史数据常因隐私或存储限制不可用。重新训练成本高昂，微调又会导致灾难性遗忘，因此需要一种能够持续适应新退化而不遗忘旧知识的方法。

Method: RwF框架采用持续域增量学习范式：1）为每个新退化学习轻量适配器，通过构造性设计消除遗忘（无需访问历史数据）；2）构建一个覆盖五个退化域、共享图像内容的基准，以隔离退化学习与数据集变化的影响；3）设计无监督路由机制，在测试时为未知输入自动选择正确的复原路径，无需领域标签。

Result: 在五域序列上，RwF在Restormer和NAFNet主干上分别比朴素顺序微调提升15.25 dB和11.83 dB的最终平均PSNR。框架迁移到11个经典真实退化基准（3,465张图像）上，路由准确率达89.5%，与Oracle（已知领域标签）相比仅有+0.94 dB的PSNR差距。

Conclusion: RwF是首个面向持续多退化图像复原的系统性基线，证明了通过轻量适配器和无监督路由机制，可以在不访问历史数据的情况下增量适应新退化并保持旧退化性能，同时与专用单域网络相比成本大幅降低。

Abstract: Recent progress in image restoration has converged on all-in-one architectures that jointly handle multiple degradations within a single network. These methods are effective on static benchmarks but target a closed-world setting that assumes simultaneous access to every target degradation at training time. In practice, degradations are encountered sequentially as field-deployed systems progressively face new environmental conditions, and historical training data is often unavailable due to privacy or storage constraints. Accommodating a new degradation then requires either retraining on the union of all prior data, which is often costly or infeasible, or fine-tuning, which causes catastrophic forgetting. We formulate multi-degradation image restoration as a continual domain-incremental learning problem, in which degradations arrive incrementally and prior data is unavailable. Our proposed Restoring without Forgetting (RwF) framework learns a lightweight adapter for each new degradation, eliminating forgetting by construction at a fraction of the cost of dedicated per-domain networks. To isolate degradation learning from dataset variation, we construct a benchmark spanning five degradation domains under shared image content. At test time, an unsupervised routing mechanism identifies the appropriate restoration path for unknown inputs without requiring domain labels. Across the five-domain sequence, RwF improves final average PSNR over naive sequential fine-tuning by 15.25 dB and 11.83 dB on the Restormer and NAFNet backbones, respectively. The framework transfers to eleven canonical real-degradation benchmarks (3,465 images) at 89.5% routing accuracy with only a +0.94 dB oracle PSNR gap, establishing, to our knowledge, the first systematic baseline for continual multi-degradation image restoration.

</details>


### [13] [LUCAID: Agentic Multimodal AI for Lung Cancer Precision Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23803)
*Marie-Lisa Eich,Kai Standvoss,Timo Milbich,Alexander Möllers,Miriam Hägele,Philipp Anders,Lars Tharun,Hanna Kontradiuk,Sebastian Kons,Nader Aldoj,Recepcan Adigüzel,Adam Narai,Lukas Hönig,Jonathan Striebel,Binru Yang,Mihnea P. Dragomir,Marvin Sextro,Philipp Keyl,Philipp Jurmeister,Rosemarie Krupar,Evelyn Ramberger,James Wells,Julika Ribbat-Idel,Andreas Kunft,Hussam Shuaib,Christian Grohé,Reinhard Büttner,David Horst,Klaus-Robert Müller,Lukas Ruff,Maximilian Alber,Frederick Klauschen,Simon Schallenberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LUCAID是一个用于精准肺癌病理学的智能体AI系统，将诊断推理与九个模块整合，覆盖从质量控制、肿瘤检测与分割、组织学亚型分类、肿瘤微环境分析、肿瘤细胞比例量化、预测性生物标志物评分（PD-L1、MET、TROP-2）到自动化结构化报告生成的全流程。模块在专家标注上实现F1分数0.82-0.95，前瞻性临床验证中与专家小组判定的参考标准达成93.0%的一致性，远超五名资深胸部病理学家的68.3-81.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌组织病理学诊断复杂，精准肿瘤学的治疗决策依赖于形态学、免疫组化和分子特征的整合，但当前病理评估仍以视觉、半定量为主，存在观察者间差异。现有AI工具仅覆盖部分任务，难以达到通用专家级性能，且缺乏前瞻性临床验证。

Method: 开发了LUCAID智能体AI系统：一个整合性智能体将诊断推理与九个模块耦合，覆盖完整常规工作流程（质量控制、肿瘤检测与分割、组织学亚型分型、肿瘤微环境分析、肿瘤细胞占比量化、预测性生物标志物评分以及自动结构化报告生成），并支持用户交互式查询模块输出和生成结果情境化报告。

Result: 针对大规模专家标注，分析模块F1分数达到0.82-0.95；在前瞻性临床验证中，LUCAID在临床可操作决策上与专家小组判定的参考标准达到93.0%的一致性，而五名资深胸部病理学家的表现仅为68.3-81.1%。

Conclusion: LUCAID作为首个经过前瞻性临床验证的智能体AI病理系统，在精准肺癌病理学的全流程任务中超越了资深病理学家的表现，展示了智能体AI在临床病理诊断中的可行性与价值。

Abstract: Lung cancer tissue diagnostics is complex, as therapy decisions in precision oncology rely on the integration of histomorphological, immunohistochemical, and molecular features. Yet pathological assessment remains largely visual and semi-quantitative and shows interobserver variability, while existing artificial intelligence (AI) tools cover only selected tasks, rarely reach generalizable expert-level performance, and lack prospective clinical validation. To address these challenges, we developed and clinically validated LUCAID, an agentic AI system for precision lung cancer pathology. An integrative agent couples diagnostic reasoning with nine modules that cover the full routine workflow, from quality control, tumor detection and segmentation, histological subtyping, tumor microenvironment profiling, tumor cellularity quantification, and predictive biomarker scoring (PD-L1, MET, TROP-2) to automated structured report generation. LUCAID enables users to interactively query the module outputs and generate reports that contextualize the results. Against large-scale expert ground-truth annotations, the analysis modules achieved F1 scores of 0.82-0.95. In prospective clinical validation, LUCAID reached 93.0% concordance with an expert-panel adjudicated reference standard across clinically actionable decisions, compared with 68.3-81.1% for five experienced thoracic pathologists.

</details>


### [14] [Predicting Radiologist Expertise from 3D Gaze Patterns During CT Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23836)
*Leila Khaertdinova,Anna Anikina,Claudia Mello-Thoms,Bulat Ibragimov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于注视信息的Transformer框架（GazeToSkill），用于胸部CT影像中的放射科医师专业水平分类。模型利用DINOv2骨干网络，通过自注意力中的可学习对数空间偏置和注视加权池化，将放射科医师的注视模式融入体素特征学习，在182次CT阅读会话上取得ROC-AUC 0.91和F1分数0.86，优于改编的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 眼动追踪已在2D医学影像中广泛研究，但在CT三维体数据场景下用于专业水平评估仍较有限。准确解读CT需要高效的3D体数据导航能力以及对诊断相关区域的关注，因此需要一种能够利用视觉搜索行为进行客观专业水平评估的方法。

Method: 提出注视引导的Transformer框架：1) 使用DINOv2作为骨干网络提取体素特征；2) 在自注意力机制中引入可学习的对数空间偏置来编码放射科医师的注视位置；3) 采用注视加权的patch嵌入池化，使注视信息贯穿体素特征学习过程。

Result: 在182次CT阅读会话（来自5位不同经验水平的放射科医师）上训练和评估，在留出测试集上达到ROC-AUC 0.91和F1分数0.86，优于改编的现有方法。

Conclusion: 将视觉搜索行为融入Transformer架构可能支持放射学中客观的、基于过程的专业水平评估，为3D医学影像场景下利用眼动数据进行技能评估提供了新思路。

Abstract: Accurate interpretation of volumetric CT requires efficient navigation of 3D image volumes and attention to diagnostically relevant regions. While eye-tracking has been widely studied in 2D medical imaging, its use for expertise assessment in CT settings remains limited. We propose a gaze-informed transformer framework for expertise classification in thoracic CT. Using a DINOv2 backbone, radiologist fixation patterns are integrated into volumetric feature learning through (1) a learnable log-space bias in self-attention and (2) gaze-weighted pooling of patch embeddings. We trained and evaluated our approach on 182 CT reading sessions from five radiologists with varying levels of experience. On a held-out test set, the model achieves an ROC-AUC of 0.91 and F1 score of 0.86, outperforming adapted methods. These findings suggest that incorporating visual search behavior into transformers may support objective, process-based expertise assessment in radiology. Code is available via https://github.com/leiluk1/GazeToSkill.

</details>


### [15] [Infant Care Video Dataset for Classification of Interventions Using Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23838)
*Igor Bogdanov,James Green*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了新生儿重症监护室(NICU)婴儿护理视频数据集(ICVD)，包含4,144个视频、12类模拟护理干预类别，用于开发自动文档记录系统。基于人体模型的方法系统性地变化拍摄角度和临床医生肤色等条件，同时确保隐私合规。使用视频Transformer架构(TimeSformer和MotionFormer)分别达到93.97%和93.17%的top-1准确率，而逐帧方法仅达23.17%，充分验证了时间建模的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NICU中的医疗文档记录面临重大挑战，护士约花费25%的工作时间在记录上，且高达60%的干预操作未被记录。因此需要从视频中自动检测护理干预行为，以减少临床负担并改进现有记录实践。

Method: 构建ICVD视频数据集，包含4,144个视频和12个模拟干预类别；采用人体模型方案系统性地变化拍摄角度和临床医生肤色等条件以保证隐私合规；使用视频Transformer架构(TimeSformer和MotionFormer)建立基线性能，并通过消融研究对比时间模型与逐帧方法。

Result: TimeSformer达到93.97%的top-1准确率，MotionFormer达到93.17%的top-1准确率；逐帧方法仅达到23.17%的准确率，两者之间存在70.80%的性能差距，验证了时间建模的显著优势。

Conclusion: ICVD数据集为开发自动化文档记录系统提供了基础，有望减少新生儿护理环境中的临床负担并改进现有实践，同时验证了视频时间建模在护理干预检测中的关键作用。

Abstract: Healthcare documentation in the neonatal intensive care unit (NICU) presents significant challenges, with nurses spending approximately 25\% of their time on record-keeping, while up to 60\% of interventions remain undocumented. Motivated by the need to detect interventions from video automatically, we present the Infant Care Video Dataset (ICVD), a collection of 4,144 videos spanning 12 simulated intervention classes designed for developing automated documentation systems. Our manikin-based approach systematically varies conditions, such as camera angle and clinician skin tone, while ensuring privacy compliance. Using video transformer architectures (TimeSformer and MotionFormer), we establish strong baseline performance (93.97\% and 93.17\% top-1 accuracy) among the 12 infant care classes. Our ablation study comparing temporal models with a framewise approach (23.17\% accuracy) demonstrates a 70.80\% performance gap, validating the need for temporal modeling. The ICVD provides a foundation for developing automated documentation systems to reduce clinical burden in neonatal care environments and improve existing practices.

</details>


### [16] [Object Counting Across Modalities: Taxonomies, Benchmarks, Applications, and Open Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23845)
*Joana Konadu Owusu,Shivanand Venkanna Sheshappanavar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了开放词汇目标计数方法的最新进展，指出其宣称的通用性远超现有评估基础设施的能力。作者引入五轴分类法（模态、机制、提示、监督层级、泛化设置）来审计文献，将当前挑战归纳为六种结构性矛盾，并提出面向组合场景理解、主动计数智能体和统一多模态评估协议的路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标计数方法从类别特定的密度回归迅速转向开放词汇、基于基础模型的计数方法，但声称的通用性超前于评估基础设施。多数进展指标依赖少数饱和基准，模型可利用统计规律走捷径；新诊断数据集揭示了语义接地、时间身份和空间推理（含遮挡）上的系统性失效。因此亟需建立稳健的评估基础设施，以区分开放世界泛化与基准特定优化。

Method: 作者提出一个五轴分类法（模态、机制、提示、监督层级、泛化设置），并利用该分类法系统审计跨应用领域（显微成像、遥感、人群计数、农业）的文献，将普遍挑战正式化为六种结构性矛盾。

Result: 该综述识别了现有计数模型在语义接地、时间身份识别和遮挡空间推理方面的系统性失败，并通过五轴分类法揭示了文献中的模式，将挑战归纳为六种结构性矛盾，进而提出发展路线图。

Conclusion: 核心要务是构建稳健的评估基础设施，以区分开放世界泛化和基准特定的优化，而非简单进行增量式工程改进。未来方向包括组合场景理解、主动计数智能体以及统一的多模态评估协议。

Abstract: Object-counting methods have rapidly shifted from class-specific density regression to open-vocabulary, foundation-model-backed counters. These methods now enumerate instances from various visual and textual prompts. While this shift marks major conceptual progress, our survey argues that claims of universal generality have outpaced the evaluative infrastructure. Most progress metrics rely on a few saturated benchmarks that models exploit for statistical regularities. Newly introduced diagnostic datasets reveal systematic failures in semantic grounding, temporal identity, and spatial reasoning with occlusion. To address these failures, we introduce a five-axis taxonomy (modality, mechanism, prompting, supervision level, and generalization setting). We use this taxonomy to audit the literature across application domains, including microscopy, remote sensing, crowd counting, and agriculture. This formalizes prevailing challenges into six structural contradictions. From these, we propose a roadmap for compositional scene understanding, active counting agents, and unified multimodal evaluation protocols. The main imperative is to build a robust evaluation infrastructure to distinguish open-world generalization from benchmark-specific optimization, rather than simple incremental engineering.

</details>


### [17] [DDMS: Discriminative Distillation of Multi-view Foundational Features into Single-view Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23850)
*Jeong-gi Kwak,Sho Kagami,Yuki Ono,Kwang Moo Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种判别式蒸馏框架，将多视图模型中的3D几何知识重新蒸馏到单视图估计器中，从而获得增强的3D一致基础特征。通过融合预训练的2D基础特征与多视图几何特征构建教师模型，并用判别式排序目标进行精炼，使学习到的特征兼具3D一致性、局部区分性，同时保持与原基础模型特征空间的对齐以保留语义结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DINO等基础视觉特征在计算机视觉中至关重要，也是多视图前馈几何估计器的关键组件。然而这些特征主要从2D数据中学习，缺乏明确的3D几何一致性，限制了其在3D视觉任务中的表现。作者希望通过蒸馏多视图模型的3D几何知识来增强单视图特征的3D一致性。

Method: 1) 构建多视图教师模型：融合预训练2D基础特征与多视图几何特征；2) 使用判别式排序目标精炼融合表示；3) 通过判别式蒸馏框架训练单视图估计器，使特征同时满足3D一致性、局部区分性，并与原始基础模型特征空间对齐以保留语义结构。

Result: 实验覆盖三个角度：直接特征分析、密集预测迁移、显式3D提升与渲染。方法在各方面均产生更强的3D感知基础特征，改善了多视图一致性和局部区分性，同时保留了原始表示的语义可迁移性。

Conclusion: 通过重蒸馏多视图模型的3D几何知识到单视图估计器，可以获得增强的3D一致基础特征，这些特征在多视图一致性与局部区分性上表现更优，且不损失语义可迁移性，为3D视觉任务提供了更强的基础特征表示。

Abstract: Foundational visual features such as DINO have played a critical role across modern computer vision, and have recently become key components in multi-view feed-forward geometry estimators. In this work, we demonstrate that by re-distilling these multi-view models---their internal knowledge of 3D geometry---into a single-view estimator, we can obtain enhanced 3D consistent foundational features. Our key idea is to construct a multi-view teacher by fusing pretrained 2D foundation features with multi-view geometric features, and refining the fused representation with a discriminative ranking objective. Through our discriminative distillation framework, we enforce the learned features to be both 3D consistent and locally distinctive, while keeping them aligned with the feature space of the original foundation model to preserve the semantic structure of the pretrained representation. Consistency and local discriminability are critical for 3D computer vision problems such as forming semantic and geometric correspondences across images. To demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, we perform comprehensive experiments spanning multiple angles: direct feature analysis, dense prediction transfer, and explicit 3D lifting and rendering. Across these evaluations, our method consistently produces stronger 3D-aware foundation features that improve multi-view consistency and local discriminability while preserving the semantic transferability of the original representation.

</details>


### [18] [LUX: A Lesion-Aware Graph-Conditioned Visual - Language Architecture for Explainable Endoscopic Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23853)
*Alexis Ivan Escamilla-Lopez,Gilberto Ochoa-Ruiz,Salvador Hinojosa,Sharib Ali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LUX，一种图条件视觉语言架构，用于可解释的内窥镜图像描述生成。LUX通过从Grad-CAM和CBAM激活图构建病灶中心场景图，将病理区域编码为节点并整合进T5解码器的交叉注意力层，实现生成词对特定病灶节点的注意力对齐，显著提升了各评估指标并减少了幻觉性临床发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 溃疡性结肠炎的内窥镜图像解读复杂且主观，人为评估存在变异性，且黏膜炎症细节难以捕捉。虽然深度学习推动了自动化分析，但现有视觉语言模型依赖全局视觉嵌入，忽略了病理证据的局部性和关系性，限制了临床可靠性和可解释性。

Method: LUX构建病灶中心场景图：从Grad-CAM和CBAM激活图中提取病理区域作为节点，编码其空间与临床关系；将图嵌入整合到T5解码器的交叉注意力层，使生成词能关注特定病灶节点而非仅全局图像特征，实现词级可解释性和关系推理。

Result: LUX在BLEU、METEOR、ROUGE-L和CIDEr指标上均优于强基线和最先进的医学描述生成模型，尤其在CIDEr上提升显著；同时减少了幻觉性临床发现，并通过生成词与局部病理区域的对应关系增强了病灶级对接能力。

Conclusion: LUX通过图条件化架构成功将病理证据的局部性和关系性融入视觉语言模型，实现了语言内容与病理证据的直接对齐，为内窥镜图像提供了更可靠且可解释的自动描述生成方案。

Abstract: The interpretation of endoscopic imagery in ulcerative colitis is complex and subjective, with variability in human assessment and subtle mucosal inflammation. Although deep learning has advanced automated analysis, most vision-language models rely on global visual embeddings that overlook the localized and relational nature of pathological evidence, limiting clinical reliability and interpretability.
  We introduce LUX (Lesion-aware Unified eXplainable captioning), a graph-conditioned vision-language architecture for explainable endoscopic image captioning. LUX constructs a lesion-centric scene graph from Grad-CAM and CBAM activation maps, representing pathological regions as nodes and encoding their spatial and clinical relationships. These graph embeddings are integrated into the cross-attention layers of a T5 decoder, enabling generated words to attend to specific lesion nodes rather than only to global image features. This provides direct alignment between linguistic content and pathological evidence, supporting token-level interpretability and relational reasoning.
  LUX outperforms strong baseline and state-of-the-art medical captioning models across BLEU, METEOR, ROUGE-L, and CIDEr, with particularly strong gains in CIDEr. It also reduces hallucinated clinical findings and improves lesion-level grounding through stronger correspondence between generated tokens and localized pathological regions.

</details>


### [19] [AffineTok: Semantic Affine Consistency for Diffusion-Friendly Visual Tokenizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23864)
*Junqiu Yu,Pandeng Li,Yikai Wang,Jiaxing Zhao,Yujie Wei,Kaixun Jiang,Quanhao Li,Hongtao Yu,Zhihang Liu,Zhaohe Liao,Junjie Zhou,Yun Zheng,Yu Liu,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出语义亲和一致性（SAC）作为视觉分词器的关键要求，并引入M_SAC代理指标（与生成质量Pearson相关达0.960），以及AffineTok训练方法（包含GSCT和PMSA两个组件），在ImageNet 256上实现gFID 1.21（无CFG）和1.10（有CFG）的SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉分词器越来越多地将语义监督注入潜在空间以促进扩散模型的下游生成，但语义应该如何组织来帮助去噪过程仍未得到充分探索。现有方法直接从噪声潜在变量预测干净图像的语义（平均值），忽略了真正需要对齐的是"平均干净潜在变量的语义"，两者之间存在不一致性，这可能是被忽视的关键缺失。

Method: 1) 形式化定义语义恢复目标，并证明语义恢复误差可正交分解为直接从噪声潜在变量的最优语义预测误差与两个预测之间误差之和；2) 提出语义亲和一致性（SAC）作为被忽视的缺失要求；3) 引入M_SAC作为分词器侧代理指标；4) 提出AffineTok，包含两个互补的训练组件：GSCT（全局语义协调令牌，协调干净潜在变量的语义组织）和PMSA（后验均值语义对齐，从噪声输入预测后验均值潜在变量并监督其语义）。

Result: M_SAC与SiT-XL的gFID达到0.960的Pearson相关，紧密追踪生成质量。在ImageNet 256上，AffineTok相比基线在20个epoch时降低26%的gFID；持续训练后达到新的SOTA：无CFG时gFID 1.21，有CFG时gFID 1.10。

Conclusion: 语义亲和一致性（SAC）是视觉分词器训练中被忽视但至关重要的要求，它使得去噪过程能更有效地恢复干净图像的语义内容。通过SAC引导的分词器训练（AffineTok）可以显著提升扩散生成质量，达到新的最先进水平。

Abstract: Visual tokenizers increasingly inject semantic supervision into latent spaces to make downstream diffusion easier. Yet how these semantics should be organized to facilitate denoising remains underexplored. In this paper, we define the semantic recovery objective: the denoising process should recover the semantic content of the clean image from noisy latent, and a good tokenizer should make it easier. Existing approaches train a projector to predict the semantics directly from the noisy latent. We argue that this predicts the average of clean-image semantics, whereas what really needs to be aligned is the semantics of averaged clean latents. More importantly, we demonstrate that the semantic recovery error orthogonally decomposes into the error of the optimal semantic prediction directly from the noisy latent and the error between these two predictions. We therefore identify their consistency as the missing requirement and call it Semantic Affine Consistency (SAC). To examine whether this overlooked requirement is closely related to downstream generation, we introduce M_SAC, a tokenizer-side proxy for SAC. Across the evaluated tokenizers and diffusion model scales, M_SAC closely tracks generation quality, reaching a Pearson correlation of 0.960 with SiT-XL gFID, thereby motivating SAC-guided tokenizer training. We then introduce AffineTok, which promotes SAC through two complementary, training-only components. Global Semantic Coordination Token (GSCT) coordinates the semantic organization of clean latents, keeping semantic averaging meaningful, while Posterior-Mean Semantic Alignment (PMSA) predicts posterior-mean latents from noisy inputs and supervises their semantics. On ImageNet 256, compared with the baseline, AffineTok reduces gFID by 26% at 20 epochs and, with continued training, achieves a new state-of-the-art gFID of 1.21 without classifier-free guidance and 1.10 with guidance.

</details>


### [20] [Gen2Physics: Grounding Generated 3D Meshes in Physics via Multi-View Material Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23869)
*Mauro Comi,Jordi Serrano Berbel,Kevis-Kokitsi Maninis,Philipp Henzler,Manuel Sanchez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Gen2Physics是一个统一的自动化框架，直接将生成的三维网格分解为物理材质组件，生成可立即用于物理引擎仿真的资源。它结合了微调的Vision Transformer进行材质分割、2D到3D一致性投影以及VLM引导的细化，在ABO-500和PartNet-Material基准上实现了48.3 mIoU（较之前方法提升超两倍），同时匹配体积法的质量估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成模型虽然能产生高保真度的三维网格，但这些输出缺乏交互仿真、游戏或机器人所需的物理属性。先前方法专注于与标准物理引擎不兼容的体积表示，因此需要一种直接在网格上操作、能自动赋予物理属性的方法。

Method: Gen2Physics包含三个核心组件：(1) 微调的Vision Transformer用于密集材质分割；(2) 鲁棒的2D到3D一致性投影；(3) VLM引导的细化，利用上下文推理来分配物理属性并推断内部几何（实心 vs 空心）。最终将表面补丁转换为具有不同密度的体积。

Result: 在ABO-500和PartNet-Material基准上，Gen2Physics将材质分割准确率从15.6提升到48.3 mIoU（超两倍），匹配体积法的质量估计精度，并且是唯一能输出水密性逐材质子网格的方法。

Conclusion: Gen2Physics证明了直接在网格上进行物理材质分解是可行且更优的方案，能够为生成的三维资产赋予可直接用于仿真的物理属性，同时在水密性、分割精度上超越现有方法。

Abstract: While state-of-the-art generative models produce high-fidelity 3D meshes, these outputs lack the physical properties required for interactive simulation, gaming, or robotics. We introduce Gen2Physics, a unified and automated framework that grounds generated meshes in physics by automatically decomposing them into their constituent material components. Unlike prior approaches, which focus on volumetric representations incompatible with standard physics engines, Gen2Physics operates directly on meshes to produce immediately simulation-ready assets. Our pipeline integrates a fine-tuned Vision Transformer for dense material segmentation, a robust 2D-to-3D consistency projection, and a Vision-Language Model (VLM) guided refinement that leverages contextual reasoning to assign physical properties and infer internal geometry (solid vs. hollow). By converting surface patches into volumes with distinct densities, our method enables physically plausible dynamic simulations. Experimental results on the ABO-500 and PartNet-Material benchmarks demonstrate that Gen2Physics more than doubles the material segmentation accuracy of prior physics-grounding pipelines (15.6 to 48.3 mIoU), while matching the mass-estimation accuracy of volumetric methods and being the only approach to output watertight per-material sub-meshes.

</details>


### [21] [LG-GER: Language-Guided Group Emotion Recognition via Multimodal Evidence Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23880)
*Ahmed Shehab Khan,Zhiyuan Li,Yan Tong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LG-GER提出了一种语言引导的知识蒸馏框架，利用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）为训练图像生成稠密、空间定位的情感证据（边界框+情感信号+置信度），并将这些结构化证据通过四种互补损失蒸馏到单一视觉语言模型（VLM）骨干中。推理时无需检测器、MLLM或多流融合，实现实时且资源受限场景下的群体情感识别，在两个基准数据集上达到与依赖检测和多流处理的最先进方法相当或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有群体情感识别（GER）方法依赖基于检测器的多流流水线，这些流水线仅使用图像级监督训练，缺乏对哪些区域重要以及每个区域贡献程度的指导，导致模型学习效率低下且推理成本高昂。

Method: 1) 使用MLLM为训练图像生成稠密、空间定位的结构化证据（边界框+情感信号+置信度分数）；2) 通过四种互补损失（分类、区域-文本对齐、空间情感、空间置信度回归）将这些证据蒸馏到单一VLM骨干中；3) 推理时仅需单一VLM，无需检测器、MLLM或多流融合。

Result: 在GroupEmoW和GAF 3.0两个GER基准数据集上，LG-GER取得了与依赖检测和多流推理的最先进方法竞争性或更优的结果，同时推理更加高效。

Conclusion: LG-GER证明了语言引导的知识蒸馏能够将复杂多流GER系统压缩为单一VLM骨干，在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅降低推理复杂度，使GER实际部署成为可能。

Abstract: Inferring the collective emotional state of a group of people from a single image, a task known as group emotion recognition (GER), requires integrating spatially distributed cues such as faces, poses, interactions, and scene context. Current methods rely on detector-driven multi-stream pipelines. These are trained with only image-level supervision that lacks guidance on which regions matter or how strongly each contributes. We propose LG-GER, a language-guided distillation framework that uses a multimodal large language model (MLLM) to generate dense, spatially grounded evidence, i.e., bounding boxes paired with emotion signals and confidence scores, for the training images. This structured evidence is distilled into a single vision-language model (VLM) backbone through four complementary losses: classification, region-text grounding, spatial emotion, and spatial confidence regression. At inference, LG-GER requires no detectors, no MLLM, and no multi-stream fusion, making GER practical for real-time and resource-constrained deployment. LG-GER has been evaluated on two benchmark GER datasets (GroupEmoW and GAF~3.0) and achieves competitive or superior results compared to state-of-the-art methods that require detection and multi-stream processing at inference.

</details>


### [22] [Continual Visual Learning under Evolving Semantic Concept Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23903)
*Ismail Lamaakal,Chaymae Yahyati,Yassine Maleh,Khalid El Makkaoui,Ibrahim Ouahbi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SemReWrite框架，用于处理长期视觉系统中语义概念发生演变（如分类合并、边界修订等）的场景，通过选择性更新过时的视觉-语义映射，同时保留仍然有效的知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉基础模型的适配假设数据外观变化但任务语义固定，但现实中分类体系、策略和概念定义本身会演变，导致相同的视觉证据需要不同的解释。这种"演化语义概念漂移"尚未被系统研究。

Method: SemReWrite框架包含：1）建模新旧语义规格之间的变化；2）结合语义差异与稀疏修订监督来定位受影响的视觉区域；3）使用输入相关的低秩重写机制，配合结构化语义记忆、保存和过时决策抑制。

Result: 提出EvoShift-Bench基准（覆盖ImageNet、iNaturalist、CUB-200-2011、DomainNet），引入RA、PA、OR和SRS四个评估指标。实验证明SemReWrite在修订语义学习和保留未受影响知识之间的平衡优于提示替换、常规微调、参数高效适配和持续学习策略。

Conclusion: SemReWrite有效解决了演化语义概念漂移问题，能够选择性更新过时的视觉-语义映射，同时保留仍然有效的知识，为长期视觉系统中的语义演变提供了系统性解决方案。

Abstract: Visual foundation models are commonly adapted under the assumption that the appearance of incoming data may change while the semantic meaning of the prediction task remains fixed. In long-lived visual systems, however, taxonomies, policies, and concept definitions can themselves evolve, causing the same visual evidence to require a different interpretation. We study this setting as evolving semantic concept shift and introduce SemReWrite, a framework for selectively updating obsolete visual--semantic mappings while preserving knowledge that remains valid. SemReWrite represents changes between old and revised semantic specifications, combines semantic discrepancy with sparse revised supervision to localize affected visual regions, and uses an input-dependent low-rank rewriting mechanism together with structured semantic memory, preservation, and obsolete-decision suppression. We further introduce EvoShift-Bench, spanning ImageNet, iNaturalist, CUB-200-2011, and DomainNet, with semantic transitions including class split, merge, boundary revision, insertion, partial redefinition, recurrence, and mixed semantic--appearance shift. To explicitly evaluate selective semantic revision, we introduce Rewrite Accuracy (RA) and Preservation Accuracy (PA) for affected and unaffected regions, respectively, Obsolete Retention (OR) for measuring residual outdated semantic associations, and the Selective Revision Score (SRS), which jointly summarizes rewriting and preservation performance. Experiments show that SemReWrite achieves a stronger balance between learning revised semantics and retaining unaffected knowledge than prompt replacement, conventional fine-tuning, parameter-efficient adaptation, and continual-learning strategies.

</details>


### [23] [HAP: Head-Adaptive Visual Token Pruning via Cross-Modal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23921)
*Yuanhao Sun,Huawei Ji,Yuan Jin,Cheng Deng,Luoyi Fu,Xinbing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PAQ（提示词对齐注意力质量）指标，用于评估视觉语言模型中每个注意力头与提示词的对齐程度，并基于此进行三阶段视觉token剪枝，在18个基准上达到SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对所有注意力头进行均匀平均来评估视觉token的重要性，但不同头与查询的对齐程度不同，未对齐的头会主导平均值，放大背景token并稀释细粒度特征，导致token压缩效果不佳。

Method: 首先提出PAQ指标量化每个注意力头与提示词的对齐质量；然后进行三阶段剪枝：(1) 根据目标FLOPs预算将transformer层分组并分配视觉token预算；(2) 通过PAQ加权的softmax聚合每组内的各头注意力图；(3) 根据聚合矩阵的幅值对视觉token打分并保留预算内的token。

Result: 在18个基准上达到SOTA权衡效果。在LLaVA-1.5-7B上（9个任务），仅保留5.6%的token就保持了原模型99.1%的性能，比最强基线AutoPrune高出4.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过PAQ加权而非均匀平均的注意力聚合方式，能更忠实地反映视觉token与提示词的相关性，从而实现更高效的视觉token压缩。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language Models encode high-resolution images into long visual token sequences, incurring prohibitive prefill costs. To compress them, existing methods score each visual token by averaging text-to-visual attention uniformly across all heads, which assumes every head matches the query. However, our empirical analysis shows that misaligned heads dominate the average, amplifying background tokens and drowning out fine-grained cues.
  To address this, we propose PAQ (Prompt-Grounded Attention Quality), a metric quantifying how well each head aligns the prompt with image regions. Built on PAQ, our pruning proceeds in three stages. Given a target FLOPs budget, we first partition the transformer layers into groups and allocate a visual token budget to each. Within each group, we then aggregate per-head attention maps via PAQ-weighted softmax into a group-level matrix. Finally, we score visual tokens by this matrix's magnitude and retain the allocated budget per group. By weighting heads with PAQ, our method scores tokens by attention signals that more faithfully reflect prompt relevance, rather than diluting them through uniform averaging.
  Across 18 benchmarks, our method delivers state-of-the-art trade-offs. Specifically, on LLaVA-1.5-7B (9 tasks), retaining only \textbf{5.6\%} tokens preserves \textbf{99.1\%} of the original performance, surpassing the strongest baseline AutoPrune by 4.2 points. Code is available in https://github.com/baokou-fw2/HAP.

</details>


### [24] [ROI-Gated SAHI: Content-Adaptive Slicing-Based Inference for Efficient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23923)
*Rashid Riyadh,Abd Ullah Khan,Imad Gohar,Muzammil Behzad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ROI-Gated SAHI框架，通过引入轻量级proposer定位前景区域，将切片细化限制在信息丰富的区域，以减少SAHI在背景瓦片上的计算浪费。实验表明，在稀疏场景中可实现最高6.90倍加速，但在COCO128全分割数据集上静态ROI门控性能不如Full SAHI，需配合自适应路由策略才能获得平均稳健收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAHI虽能改善高分辨率图像中的小目标检测，但大量计算被浪费在背景块上，效率低下。

Method: 提出ROI-Gated SAHI推理框架，使用轻量级proposer定位前景区域，仅对信息丰富的区域执行切片细化；并引入基于tau=0.4的自适应路由策略来控制何时启用ROI门控。

Result: COCO128全分割上静态ROI门控速度比为0.88（慢于Full SAHI），mAP@0.5为0.6602（低于Full SAHI的0.7569）；自适应路由获得1.02倍轻微加速。三图稀疏到密集案例研究中，加速范围为0.96倍至6.90倍，平均3.41倍加速。

Conclusion: ROI门控在稀疏场景中优势显著，但平均行为需要基于策略的路由才能保证稳健性，静态门控在密集场景中反而会降低性能。

Abstract: Slicing-Aided Hyper Inference (SAHI) improves small object detection in high-resolution images but often spends substantial compute on background tiles. We propose region-of-interest (ROI)-Gated SAHI, an inference-time framework that introduces a lightweight proposer to localize foreground regions and restrict sliced refinement to informative areas. We evaluate the framework in two settings. On the COCO128 full split dataset comprising 128 images, static ROI-gating is slower on average than Full SAHI, achieving a speed ratio of 0.88, and yields a lower mAP@0.5 of 0.6602 compared with 0.7569 for Full SAHI. A simple adaptive routing policy with $τ=$ 0.4 educes the mean latency, achieving a slight gain of 1.02$\times$ over Full SAHI. On a three-image sparse-to-dense case study, ROI-gating achieves speedups ranging from 0.96$\times$ to 6.90$\times$ with a mean speedup of 3.41$\times$. These results show that ROI-gating is most beneficial in sparse scenes and requires policy-based routing for robust average behavior.

</details>


### [25] [GlanceWAM: Sparse Test-Time Imagination for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23927)
*Linhan Wang,Zijian An,Mingyuan Zhang,Chen Dai,Yi Xu,Can Cui,Zichong Yang,Yinlin Chen,Lifeng Zhou,Chang-Tien Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GlanceWAM 提出了一种异步视觉想象框架，将视频生成与动作控制解耦在同一个视频 DiT 中，在关键路径之外后台生成前瞻帧，而动作头以控制频率在潜在空间中直接解码，从而同时实现实时推理（48ms/块）和高任务成功率（RoboCasa 72.2%，LIBERO 99.0%），突破了速度-成功率的两难困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界动作模型（WAMs）存在根本性权衡：同步视频生成虽然能利用丰富的物理先验，但控制频率下的推理延迟过高；而放弃测试时的视觉想象则牺牲任务成功率。因此需要一种既能实时推理又能保持高成功率的方案。

Method: GlanceWAM 核心方法包括：(1) 将想象与控制解耦在单个视频 DiT 中，异步提议器在慢时钟上后台生成数秒后的单个前瞻帧，动作头在潜在空间以控制频率（48ms）解码动作块；(2) 非干扰注意力掩码隔离视频表示，防止干扰；(3) 稳健性-容忍水平训练适应异步前瞻帧的时效衰减。

Result: 在仅使用演示数据训练的条件下，GlanceWAM 在 24 任务 RoboCasa 厨房基准上达到 72.2% 成功率（超过同步 Cosmos Policy 的 67.1% 和无想象共训练的 64.4%），在 LIBERO 上达到 99.0%，每个块仅需 48ms（在 NVIDIA A100 上比同步基线快 24 倍）。

Conclusion: 异步生成视觉想象并直接在潜在空间消费，可以在不牺牲任务成功率的前提下实现实时推理，成功打破了速度与成功率之间的权衡，为视频生成模型在机器人学习中的应用提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Video generative models provide rich physical priors for robot learning, yet existing world-action models (WAMs) face a fundamental trade-off: synchronous video generation at control rate is latency-prohibitive, while abandoning test-time visual imagination sacrifices task success. We show that visual imagination achieves both real-time inference and superior success rates when generated asynchronously off the critical path and consumed directly in latent space. We introduce GlanceWAM, which decouples imagination from control within a single video DiT: an asynchronous proposer glances ahead on a slow clock to imagine a single lookahead frame seconds into the future in the background, while an action head decodes action chunks at control rate (48 ms) purely in latent space without blocking. Enabled by a non-interfering attention mask that isolates video representations and staleness-robust horizon training that accommodates asynchronous lookahead aging, GlanceWAM breaks the speed-success dilemma. Trained purely on demonstrations, it attains 72.2% on the 24-task RoboCasa kitchen benchmark (surpassing synchronous Cosmos Policy at 67.1% and imagination-free co-training at 64.4%) and 99.0% on LIBERO, executing at 48 ms per chunk on an NVIDIA A100 GPU (24x faster than synchronous baselines). Code is available at https://github.com/linhanwang/GlanceWAM.

</details>


### [26] [RefineRank: Joint Box Refinement and Ranking for Surgical Spatio-Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23928)
*Linzhe Jiang,Jiayuan Huang,Changhao Zhang,Chunyang Jiang,Zhehua Mao,Mobarak I. Hoque*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RefineRank 是一个轻量级模块，用于手术时空定位（STG）任务。它通过一个紧凑的可训练模块 RefineNet，将冻结的医学视觉语言模型和开放集检测器的候选框特征相结合，预测坐标校正和置信度分数，从而在不重新训练任何骨干网络的前提下，实现问题理解与精确定位的调和。在 MedVidBench 官方排行榜上取得 0.421 的 STG mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的手术时空定位方法面临两难困境：视觉语言模型理解问题上下文但坐标不精确，而开放集检测器能提供局部候选框但其置信度不反映哪个框真正回答问题。需要弥合这两者之间的差距。

Method: 提出 RefineRank，在候选框层面弥合差距。核心是紧凑可训练的模块 RefineNet，它将冻结的医学视觉语言模型的语言和区域特征，与冻结的开放集检测器提出的候选框相结合。RefineNet 为每个候选框预测有界的坐标校正和质量分数，并通过固定解码规则返回得分最高的原始或精化框。

Result: 在 MedVidBench 官方排行榜（已验证）上取得 0.421 STG mIoU，为显示的最高 STG 分数，全局多指标排名第 11。在受控实验中，坐标校正将候选框oracle上界从 0.6772 提升至 0.7302；联合排序原始和精化候选框使 STG mIoU 从 0.2719 提升至 0.4534，优于单独训练的选择器（最高 0.4186）。

Conclusion: 证明了一个小的框级模块可以在不重新训练任一骨干网络的情况下，调和问题理解与精确定位，为手术时空定位任务提供了一种高效且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Surgical spatio-temporal grounding (STG) requires locating, at each video time specified by a procedural question, the object that the question asks about. Existing approaches face a trade-off: vision language models understand the question context but produce imprecise coordinates, whereas open-set detectors provide localized candidate boxes whose confidence does not reflect which box answers the question. We introduce RefineRank, which closes this gap at the candidate-box level. A compact trainable module, RefineNet, combines the language and regional features of a frozen medical vision language model with the proposals of a frozen open-set detector: it predicts a bounded coordinate correction and a quality score for every candidate box, and a fixed decoding rule returns the original or refined box with the highest score. On the MedVidBench Official Rankings (Verified), RefineRank records 0.421 STG mIoU, the highest displayed STG score, while its global multi-metric rank is 11. In a controlled evaluation on separate training and evaluation videos, coordinate correction raises the candidate oracle upper bound from 0.6772 to 0.7302, and ranking the joint pool of original and refined candidates by their RefineNet scores improves STG mIoU from 0.2719 to 0.4534, whereas separately trained selectors over the same pool reach at most 0.4186. These results show that a small box-level module can reconcile question understanding with precise localization without retraining either backbone. Code is available at [https://github.com/linzhe001/RefineRank](https://github.com/linzhe001/RefineRank).

</details>


### [27] [SceneReGen: Generative Reconstruction of 3D Scenes from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23930)
*Zefan Tian,Yuteng Ye,Yiheng Zhang,Yuhang Yang,Xueqiang Lv,Shizhou Zhang,Le Liu,Di Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneReGen提出了一种生成式重建框架，将单图像3D场景重建重新定义为在共享的观测对齐场景坐标系中生成和组装完整物体资产的过程。通过选择性位姿分解（在生成的网格中直接编码观察方向，同时从实例级和全局场景证据估计平移和尺度），该方法弥合了物体生成与场景重建之间的表示差距。在3D-FUTURE评估子集上取得了最优的场景级CD、F-Score和3D框IoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图像3D场景重建需要同时完成部分观测物体的重建，并将它们连贯地放置在共享的观测对齐场景坐标系中。虽然物体级生成先验具有强大的补全能力，但其输出通常是以物体为中心、尺度归一化的物体坐标系表示，与场景重建所需的场景坐标系之间存在根本性的表示差距（representation gap）。

Method: SceneReGen通过选择性位姿分解来解决生成-重建差距：物体的观测方向直接编码到生成的网格中，而平移和尺度则从实例级和全局场景证据估计。框架包含一个几何编码器提取稠密线索，可学习的形状查询（shape queries）条件化一个预训练的基于DiT的3D生成器以生成处于观测方向下的完整网格，同时位置查询（position queries）融合物体和场景特征以在共享坐标系中组装它们。

Result: 在3D-FUTURE评估子集上，SceneReGen在场景级CD、场景级F-Score和3D边界框IoU上达到最优，与最优方法持平于物体级CD，并在物体级F-Score上排名第二。

Conclusion: SceneReGen展示了以资产为中心（asset-centric）的重建思路的有效性，在自动驾驶和具身AI场景中的定性结果进一步表明该方法在室内家具之外的广泛适用性和潜力。

Abstract: Single-image 3D scene reconstruction must complete partially observed objects and place them coherently in a shared observation-aligned scene frame. Object-level generative priors offer strong completion ability, but their centered, scale-normalized outputs are typically expressed in an object frame, creating a fundamental representation gap between object generation and scene reconstruction. We introduce SceneReGen, a generative reconstruction framework that reinterprets scene reconstruction as the generation and assembly of complete object assets in a shared observation-aligned scene frame. SceneReGen addresses the generation-reconstruction gap through selective pose factorization: each object's observed orientation is encoded directly in the generated mesh, while translation and scale are estimated from instance-level and global scene evidence. Given a scene image and instance masks, a geometry encoder extracts dense cues; learnable shape queries condition a pretrained DiT-based 3D generator to produce complete meshes in their observed orientations, while position queries fuse object and scene features to assemble them in the shared frame. On the 3D-FUTURE evaluation subset, SceneReGen achieves the best scene-level CD, scene-level F-Score, and 3D bounding-box IoU among the evaluated methods, ties the best object-level CD, and ranks second in object-level F-Score. Qualitative outputs in autonomous-driving and embodied-AI scenes further illustrate the potential of asset-centric reconstruction beyond indoor furniture.

</details>


### [28] [Luce: Relightable Gaussians for 3D Asset Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23943)
*Mayank Singh,Michele Stoppa,Alvise Memo,Rui Yu,Harsha Kalli,Srimanth Gunturi,Muhammad Ahmed Riaz,Behrooz Shahsavari,Waleed Abdulla,David E. Jacobs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Luce提出了一种统一几何与PBR材质的3D表示方法，通过体素化多模态高斯云和变分自编码器压缩到统一的材质感知潜空间，再用整流流变换器从单张图像生成潜码，实现可重光照、几何精确且材质保真的单图到3D生成，在Toys4K上FID提升28%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真图像到3D生成需要同时捕获几何与外观，且表示应支持重光照并兼容标准渲染管线（包含albedo、金属粗糙度、法线等PBR模态），现有方法在此方面存在不足。

Method: 1) 提出Luce表示：在体素化多模态高斯云中，用专门的高斯基元分别表示各模态，统一几何与PBR材质；2) 用变分自编码器（VAE）将该表示压缩为统一的材质感知潜空间；3) 训练整流流变换器（rectified-flow transformer），以预训练图像编码器的多层特征为条件（保留语义与细粒度空间细节），从单图生成潜码；4) 潜码解码为可重光照的PBR高斯及可选带切线空间法线贴图的纹理网格。

Result: 在Toys4K上达到SOTA单图像到3D生成效果，FID较最强基线提升28%；在新增的AI生成图像基准上，CLIP图像对齐分数从0.8299提升至0.8519；生成资产可重光照、几何精确、材质保真，能保留文字、标志和铭文等细节。

Conclusion: Luce证明了将PBR材质融入统一的3D高斯表示并配合材质感知潜空间生成，能够显著提升单图到3D生成的质量与可重光照能力，为可部署于标准渲染管线的3D资产生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: High-fidelity image-to-3D generation requires a 3D representation that captures both geometry and appearance. To support relighting and integration into standard rendering pipelines, the representation should include physically based rendering (PBR) modalities such as albedo, metallic-roughness, and surface normals. We propose Luce, a 3D representation that unifies geometry and PBR materials within a voxelized multimodal Gaussian cloud, using dedicated Gaussian primitives for each modality. A variational autoencoder compresses this representation into a unified material-aware latent space. A rectified-flow transformer generates this latent from a single image, conditioned on multi-layer features from a pretrained image encoder that preserve both semantic context and fine spatial detail. The latent then decodes into relightable PBR Gaussians and an optional textured mesh with a tangent-space normal map. On Toys4K, Luce achieves state-of-the-art single-image-to-3D generation, improving FID by 28% over the strongest baseline. We further introduce a benchmark of AI-generated images, on which Luce improves the CLIP image-alignment score over the best baseline (0.8519 vs. 0.8299). Luce generates relightable, geometrically accurate, and materially faithful assets that preserve fine details such as text, logos, and inscriptions.

</details>


### [29] [Boot-and-Feedback Framework for Generalist-Expert Model Collaboration in Breast Ultrasound Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23974)
*Ming Cheng,Hongyu Sun,Zhaolin Chen,Jun Liu,Hossein Rahmani,Qiuhong Ke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BooF（Boot-and-Feedback）框架，通过引导阶段让多模态大语言模型（MLLM）基于BI-RADS词典和视觉专家预测生成可信描述，再通过反馈阶段利用注意力门控跨模态融合模块将文本反馈与视觉特征结合，提升了乳腺超声诊断的准确性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺超声诊断依赖操作者经验，深度学习虽有效但缺乏可靠性和可解释性；现有MLLM因领域知识不足常生成误导性描述，影响下游专家模型的临床有效性。

Method: 提出BooF模型协作框架：1) Boot阶段：用BI-RADS词典和良恶性视觉专家预测引导MLLM生成描述，避免幻觉；2) Feedback阶段：通过轻量级注意力门控跨模态融合模块，将文本描述与视觉特征集成，使专家模型利用文本反馈同时自适应过滤噪声。

Result: 在多个乳腺超声数据集上，BooF在诊断准确性和可解释性方面显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: BooF通过MLLM与专家模型的协同交互，有效解决了MLLM幻觉问题和跨模态信息融合中的噪声干扰，提升了乳腺超声诊断的性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Breast ultrasound (BUS) is widely used for breast cancer diagnosis yet remains operator-dependent. While deep learning shows promise, ensuring diagnostic reliability and interpretability is challenging. Recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) often generate spurious descriptions due to limited domain knowledge, which mislead downstream expert models and compromise clinical validity. To address these challenges, we propose the Boot-and-Feedback (BooF) model collaboration framework for synergistic MLLM-expert interaction. Specifically, in the Boot Stage, the MLLM is guided by the BI-RADS lexicon and preliminary benign-malignant vision-expert predictions, enabling it to transfer general reasoning to BUS analysis while avoiding hallucinations. Subsequently, the Feedback Stage integrates these descriptions with visual features via a lightweight Attention-Gated Cross-Modality Fusion Module. This allows the expert to leverage textual feedback while adaptively filtering noise. Extensive experiments on multiple BUS datasets demonstrate that BooF substantially outperforms state-of-the-art methods in terms of diagnostic accuracy and interpretability.

</details>


### [30] [Source-Face Authenticity Detection for 3D Gaussian Heads Reconstructed from a Single Portrait: A Benchmark and Dedicated Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23984)
*Yujie Gao,Zijian Yu,Yan Hong,Jun Lan,Jianfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出面向单图3D高斯头部重建伪造检测的大规模基准，并设计了一种两阶段训练策略的检测器：第一阶段通过掩码自编码保留细粒度外观信息、多视图对比学习维持视图间特征一致性；第二阶段冻结骨干网络并拼接不同深度的CLS token进行分类，在多项指标上均优于现有检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图3D高斯头部重建能从单张肖像生成高度逼真且可自由渲染的数字头部，但重建与渲染过程会削弱源肖像中的伪造痕迹，使得生成的3D人脸难以判断其底层人脸是真实还是伪造，对身份认证和面部隐私构成风险。现有检测器缺乏显式机制来保留细粒度信息和维持跨渲染视图的特征一致性。

Method: 提出两阶段训练策略的检测器。Stage I中，掩码自编码(MAE)促使视觉骨干保留局部重建所需的细粒度外观信息，同时多视图对比学习强制同一头部在不同渲染视图间的特征一致性。Stage II冻结已适配的骨干网络，将低、中、高三个层次深度的CLS token拼接用于分类，原因是不同深度的CLS token展现出互补的空间注意力模式。此外还构建了包含真实肖像和多种来源伪造肖像的首个大规模基准数据集。

Result: 在提出的基准上，该方法在所有报告指标中准确率最高、排名第一，优于现有代表性检测器。

Conclusion: 通过保留细粒度信息和维持跨视图特征一致性，两阶段训练策略能有效提升对3D高斯重建人脸的伪造检测能力，为这一新出现的深度伪造威胁提供了可靠的防御方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in single-image 3D Gaussian head reconstruction have enabled highly realistic and freely renderable digital heads from a single portrait. However, reconstruction and rendering can weaken the forgery traces in the source portrait, making the resulting 3D face difficult to classify whether its underlying face is real or fake, and thereby posing risks to identity authentication and face privacy. To study this problem, we introduce the first large-scale benchmark for this task by collecting real portraits and fake portraits from multiple sources and evaluate representative existing detectors on this benchmark, revealing their lack of explicit mechanisms for retaining fine-grained information and maintaining feature consistency across rendered views. To directly address these two limitations, we propose a detector trained with a two-stage strategy. In Stage I, masked autoencoding encourages the visual backbone to retain the fine-grained appearance information required for local reconstruction, while multi-view contrastive learning enforces feature consistency across rendered views of the same head. Since CLS tokens at different depths exhibit complementary spatial attention patterns, Stage II freezes the adapted backbone and concatenates low-, middle-, and high-level CLS tokens for classification. Experiments show that our method achieves the highest accuracy and ranks first across all reported metrics among the evaluated detectors.

</details>


### [31] [Absorbing Gradient Conflicts: Modeling Semantic Variance via Kent Distributions for Cross-Modal Hashing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24010)
*Hengjie Zhu,Dayan Wu,Zihao Zhang,Xinze Liu,Jingxuan Yu,Peng Fu,Zheng Lin,Weiping Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出KDPH框架，将跨模态哈希中的类别代理从确定性点表示升级为超球面上的各向异性Kent分布，通过动态调整方向方差来吸收多标签场景下的梯度冲突，避免优化崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督代理式跨模态哈希方法将类别代理建模为嵌入空间中的确定性点，这种刚性假设在多标签场景下会因标签共现导致严重的梯度冲突、梯度竞争和优化崩溃问题。

Method: 1) 将代理从静态点转换为超球面上的各向异性Kent分布，通过动态调整方向方差来吸收梯度冲突，同时保持语义均值方向稳定；2) 推导结合Cayley变换的定制损失函数，确保几何参数的严格正交性以实现稳定训练。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，KDPH能有效缓解代理坍缩和混沌振荡问题，显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: KDPH是首个将Kent分布成功引入跨模态哈希的框架，为多标签跨模态检索中的代理建模提供了新的分布视角，代码已开源。

Abstract: Supervised proxy-based deep cross-modal hashing has become the dominant paradigm for large-scale retrieval. However, prevalent methods model class proxies as deterministic points in the embedding space. This rigid assumption causes severe gradient conflicts in multi-label scenarios, where gradient conflicts arising from label co-occurrence lead to severe gradient contention and optimization collapse. To resolve this, we propose Kent-based Distributional Proxy Hashing (KDPH), a novel framework that shifts proxy representation from static points to flexible anisotropic Kent distributions on the hypersphere. Unlike point proxies that must shift their positions to accommodate conflicting gradients, KDPH absorbs these conflicts by dynamically adjusting its directional variance. This allows the proxy to maintain a stable semantic mean direction while stretching to cover diverse label correlations. Furthermore, to ensure stable training of these geometric parameters, we derive a tailored loss function incorporating the Cayley transform to enforce strict orthogonality. To the best of our knowledge, KDPH is the first framework to successfully introduce the Kent distributions into cross-modal hashing. Experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that KDPH mitigates proxy collapse and chaotic oscillation, significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Code is available at https://github.com/Senmo996/KDPH-official-code.

</details>


### [32] [IterCAD: Iterative Program Repair for CAD Code Generation from Orthographic Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24020)
*Yuchuan Wu,Ke Niu,Haiyang Yu,Zhuofan Chen,Xiangyang Xue,Bin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IterCAD是一个迭代式框架，将正交视图到CAD代码的生成问题重构为渐进式程序修复过程。它通过反复分析当前CAD结果、推理与目标视图的差异，并明确决定是修正代码还是停止细化，配合IterCAD-RS监督集和三阶段训练策略，在CADExpert基准上显著提升了代码可执行性和几何保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言方法将正交视图到CAD代码生成视为一次性生成任务，模型无法检查中间CAD结果并纠正早期错误，导致输出代码不可执行或几何不一致。作者希望通过迭代修复的方式弥补这一缺陷。

Method: 1) 提出IterCAD迭代框架，将生成过程转化为渐进式程序修复；2) 构建IterCAD-RS结构化监督集，包含可修复的中间CAD状态和已正确的状态；3) 开发三阶段训练策略：初始生成、修订学习、多轮强化学习优化。

Result: 在CADExpert基准上，IterCAD相比强一次性基线方法，持续提升了代码可执行性和几何保真度。

Conclusion: 通过闭环视觉理解、几何验证和代码细化，IterCAD能够渐进式地修正结构和参数错误，验证了迭代修复范式在CAD生成任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Generating executable parametric CAD code from dimension-annotated orthographic drawings is a challenging task requiring geometric understanding, procedural reasoning, and precise numerical prediction. Existing vision-language approaches typically formulate this problem as one-shot generation, preventing the model from inspecting intermediate CAD results and correcting early mistakes, often leading to non-executable code or geometrically inconsistent outputs. In this paper, we propose IterCAD, an iterative framework that reformulates orthographic-view-to-CAD generation as a progressive program repair process. Instead of predicting the final CAD code in a single pass, IterCAD repeatedly analyzes the current CAD result, reasons about its discrepancy with the target views, and explicitly decides whether to REVISE the code or STOP the refinement process. To make iterative repair learnable, we further construct IterCAD-RS, a structured revise-or-stop supervision set containing both repairable intermediate CAD states and already-correct states, and develop a three-stage training strategy for initial generation, revision learning, and multi-turn RL optimization. By closing the loop between visual understanding, geometric verification, and code refinement, IterCAD progressively corrects structural and parametric errors. Experiments on CADExpert show that IterCAD consistently improves code executability and geometric fidelity over strong one-shot baselines.

</details>


### [33] [Low-Rank Velocity Fields as a Structural Prior for Unsupervised 4D Medical Image Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24025)
*Haojin Li,Hengzhuo Wang,Chang Liu,Zhiheng Ma,Heng Li,Jiang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于低秩速度场先验的无监督4D医学图像插值方法。通过Tucker低秩参数化约束运动场，配合粗到细多尺度建模，在仅有端点监督的条件下合成中间帧，在ACDC和4D-Lung数据集上达到SOTA性能，接近有中间帧监督的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端点-only无监督4D医学图像插值问题中，仅有起始和结束帧可用于训练，弱约束设置常导致生成的中间帧出现不稳定边界和非生理运动，限制了可解释性和下游分析。现有方法缺乏有效的结构先验来约束运动场。

Method: 1) 将运动约束在Tucker低秩速度场空间中，将运动分解为全局共享的空间基和紧凑的样本特定核心；2) 采用粗到细多尺度方案建模运动，捕获全局协调与局部非刚性细节；3) 推理时将各尺度变形组合以合成任意时刻的体积；4) 提供理论分析证明Tucker参数化下低秩参数控制速度场的光滑性能量。

Result: 在ACDC和4D-Lung数据集上达到SOTA性能，与有中间帧监督训练的方法相比具有竞争力，生成的中间帧具有更好的结构一致性和更稳定的解剖轮廓。

Conclusion: 低秩速度场作为一种有效的结构先验，能够在弱监督条件下促进更平滑、更符合解剖学一致性的变形，提升端点-only无监督4D医学图像插值的质量。

Abstract: Endpoint-only unsupervised 4D medical image interpolation synthesizes intermediate volumes from sparsely sampled sequences with only the start and end volumes available for training; however, this weakly constrained setting often yields intermediates with unstable boundaries and non-physiological motion, limiting interpretability and downstream analysis. We propose low-rank velocity fields as a structural prior, constraining motion to a structured Tucker low-rank velocity field space that decomposes motion into globally shared spatial bases and a compact sample-specific core, thereby encouraging spatially correlated, anatomy-consistent deformation while suppressing voxel-wise high-frequency artifacts. To capture global coordination and local non-rigid details, we model motion in a coarse-to-fine multi-scale scheme and compose scale-wise deformations at inference to synthesize volumes at arbitrary times. We further provide a theoretical analysis showing that, under Tucker parameterization, low-rank parameters control the smoothness energy of the velocity field, explaining why low-rank modeling promotes smoother motion. Experiments on ACDC and 4D-Lung demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, remaining competitive with methods trained with intermediate-frame supervision, and producing intermediates with improved structural coherence and more stable anatomical contours.

</details>


### [34] [Phase-Aligned Finite-Fourier Periodic Deformation for 4D Medical Image Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24027)
*Haojin Li,Hengzhuo Wang,Zhiheng Ma,Mingyang Ou,Heng Li,Jiang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种4D医学图像插值方法，将插值问题建模为带相位结构先验的连续变形过程，通过傅里叶基相位条件速度场和相位对齐的时间重参数化，有效处理准周期运动和非均匀运动，在ACDC和4D-Lung数据集上取得了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D医学图像插值（如心脏MRI和胸部CT）中的运动往往是重复性或准周期性的，但这种结构并未直接编码在形变表示中；同时生理运动通常是非均匀的，相等的时间间隔不代表相等的解剖变化量。

Method: 将插值问题形式化为学习带相位结构先验的连续形变过程。具体包括：(1) 使用有限傅里叶基参数化相位条件速度场，将近周期运动模式直接嵌入形变空间并支持任意目标时间的连续查询；(2) 引入相位对齐的时间重参数化，根据形变变化强度将归一化时间映射到潜在运动相位，以建模非均匀运动；(3) 通过双向翘曲端点、双向融合和轻量残差细化来合成中间体。

Result: 在ACDC和4D-Lung数据集上的实验表明，该方法相比现有基线取得了最先进的性能，能够从稀疏观测中生成解剖学上合理且连贯的中间体积。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了将相位结构先验嵌入连续形变空间对4D医学图像插值的有效性，尤其在处理准周期性和非均匀运动方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: 4D medical image interpolation aims to recover missing volumes from sparsely observed time points and is important for dynamic anatomical analysis in applications such as cardiac MRI and thoracic CT, where motion is often repetitive or near-periodic over clinically relevant intervals. A key challenge is that this structure is not always encoded directly in deformation representations for interpolation. In addition, physiological motion is often non-uniform, so equal temporal intervals do not necessarily correspond to equal amounts of anatomical change. To address these issues, we formulate interpolation as learning a continuous deformation process with a phase-structured prior. Given two endpoint volumes, we parameterize a phase-conditioned velocity field with a finite Fourier basis, which embeds near-periodic motion patterns directly into the deformation space and supports continuous querying at arbitrary target times. We further introduce a phase-aligned temporal reparameterization that maps normalized within-interval time to a latent motion phase according to deformation variation intensity, thereby better modeling non-uniform motion progression. Intermediate volumes are then synthesized by continuously warping both endpoints, followed by bidirectional fusion and lightweight residual refinement. Experiments on ACDC and 4D-Lung show that the proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance over existing baselines while producing anatomically plausible and coherent intermediate volumes from sparse observations.

</details>


### [35] [ConsensusTAS: Self-Supervised Temporal Action Segmentation for Long-Horizon Construction Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24043)
*Xiaoshan Zhou,Yafei Sun*
